# Bearly Manual — Full

> The complete Bearly Manual in one Markdown document: every setting and capability, how they connect, and tested recipes that combine them.

Index: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual.md](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual.md)

## Manual directory

### Start Here

- [Bearly Manual](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual.md): The complete guide to using Bearly, covering every setting and capability, how features connect, and step-by-step recipes that combine them, for people, AI assistants, and agents.
- [What Bearly is](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/what-is-bearly.md): The facts about Bearly in one place, including what it does, who it's for, which models and platforms it covers, how plans and privacy work, and where to go next.
- [Get Bearly on every device](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly.md): Where Bearly runs (web app, phone browsers, desktop app, iOS app, Chrome extension), how to install and sign in on each, what the desktop app adds, and a table of which capabilities work on which platform.
- [Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md): Bearly's plans and prices, which features each plan unlocks, how AI credits and the usage meter work, what you see at a limit, Credit Boost, and how to change, cancel, or restore a plan bought on the web, in the iOS app, or as an organization seat.
- [Tour the workspace](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/workspace-tour.md): A map of Bearly's screens and where each control lives — the sidebar and account menu, the New Chat launcher, the chat header, thread, composer, and context strip, the command menu, the Settings window, the other pages, and what changes in the desktop app, on phones, and in the iOS app.

### Chat

- [Write, attach, and send](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/compose.md): How to write and send messages in Bearly, attach files and photos, unlock password-protected PDFs, share your screen, call tools and files with / and @, queue follow-ups while Bearly works, stop or interrupt an answer, and what the PII Blocker redacts.
- [Choose and compare models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md): How to choose, compare, and switch AI models in Bearly, including the model picker, Bearly Fast, Pro, and Ultra, defaults by plan and usage, per-chat and new-chat defaults, plan-gated and hidden models, Zero Data Retention only, running out of credits, and comparing answers with Rerun and Council of Experts.
- [Work with answers](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/answers.md): How to copy, edit, delete, rerun, and compare versions of messages in Bearly, read answers aloud or open them in Canvas, read Thinking steps and citations, what answers can display, what to do when an answer fails, and how to move around long chats with the timeline and chapters.
- [Organize, find, share, and export chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md): How to start, rename, pin, archive, delete, select, and move chats into projects in Bearly, read the sidebar, use Needs attention, search chats and files, use the command menu, download a chat as Markdown, share a chat as a Page, ask Bearly to manage chats for you, and open What's New.
- [Tune a chat: settings, style, reasoning, and memory](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/settings.md): Every Bearly chat setting explained, including This Chat versus New Chats, which setting wins when projects and organizations set their own, Response Style, Reasoning Effort, Temperature, Instructions, Language, Web Search, History modes, Memories, Personalization, and how Bearly tells the AI today's date.
- [Talk to Bearly: dictation and voice calls](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md): How to speak to Bearly instead of typing, with push-to-talk dictation into the message box and live voice calls that can run actions, plus Read aloud, every Dictation and Voice setting, plan and platform requirements, and limits.
- [Keyboard shortcuts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/shortcuts.md): Every Bearly keyboard shortcut for macOS and for Windows and Linux, covering sending, new lines, interrupting, the / and @ picker, the command menu, search, new and deleted chats, jumping between prompts, Settings, dialogs, dictation, and the desktop app's global shortcut.

### Capabilities

- [Research the web, papers, people, products, and places](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md): Which Bearly research tool fits a job, and how to start, steer, and limit Web Search, Read Site, Image Search, Deep Research, Scholar, Council of Experts, Best Pick, People Search, SEO data, Domain research, Maps, Weather, and X, with plans, admin controls, Routines support, and tested combinations.
- [Learn from YouTube, online video, and posts on X](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/videos-and-posts.md): How to open, summarize, cut, quote, and fact-check YouTube videos in Bearly, save videos from YouTube and other sites into a chat with Get Video, and search and read public posts, threads, videos, and profiles on X.
- [Work with files and documents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/documents.md): How Bearly opens, previews, searches, cites, summarizes, edits, converts, and proofreads files in a chat or project, including supported file types, size limits, password-protected PDFs, Always Search Documents, the Document editor, and what changes in end-to-end encrypted chats.
- [Analyze data and run code](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/data-and-code.md): How to analyze spreadsheets and files with Run Python, draw KPI, bar, and line charts with Charts, and pick the right visual with Visualize in Bearly, including what the Python sandbox includes, file inputs and outputs, chart limits, and how these combine with documents and Pages.
- [Make Pages and edit in Canvas](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/pages-and-canvas.md): How to build interactive Pages in Bearly, refine them through versions, prepare and publish them to a stable bearly.sh link with the right audience, share a conversation's key takeaways, and edit an answer as a document in Canvas, with every limit that applies.
- [Make images, video, and audio](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/images-video-audio.md): How to generate and edit images, make and edit short videos, turn text into speech with Read aloud and Voice Studio, transcribe audio and video, and have Bearly look at images and video, with each tool's options, limits, and admin controls.
- [Record and use meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md): How to record online meetings, in-person conversations, and voice notes in Bearly's Meetings and Recordings page, follow the live transcript, get the final transcript and speaker names, summarize, export, and hand a transcript to a chat or project to draft follow-ups.
- [Let Bearly use a browser, your files, and your computer](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md): How a Bearly desktop chat browses sites in Bearly Browser, works in your signed-in Chrome tabs, reads and edits files and runs commands with Work locally, and operates apps with Computer Use, including the approvals each asks for and how to choose between them.
- [Connect apps and add skills](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/connectors-and-skills.md): How to connect services and your own MCP servers to Bearly, turn a connector on for a chat or a routine, sign in on each device, and install and run skills, including the built-in Visualize and Bearly AI Models skills, with platform limits and approvals.
- [Automate with Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md): How to create, schedule, equip, and deliver Bearly routines, including the Pro requirement, where routines run, exactly which tools a scheduled run can use, connectors, skills, Deep Research, permission mode, unattended local access, browser access, the notification filter, email and text delivery, and run history.
- [Send email and texts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/email-and-text.md): How Bearly emails you or your teammates and texts your verified phone from a chat or a routine, how to set up Phone & SMS, who can receive messages, attachment and rate limits, and what to do when a send fails.
- [Approvals, permissions, and Full access](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/permissions.md): How Bearly asks before using tools outside the chat, how long each approval lasts, how to review and revoke saved approvals in the Permissions control, how Standard and Full access differ, what always asks, starting new chats in Full access, organization Agent access, routine permission mode, and spoken approvals.

### Combine

- [Building blocks: how Bearly's features connect](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/building-blocks.md): The composition model behind Bearly — what each capability takes in and produces, which outputs feed which tools, where each block runs, which plans and Routines support it, and what breaks a chain — so a person or an AI can design new multi-step workflows.
- [Recipes for research and analysis](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/research-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that chain Bearly's research tools (Deep Research, Scholar, Read Site, document search, YouTube claim checks, X posts, Rerun, and Council of Experts) with Run Python, charts, Pages, and email to produce cited reports, checked figures, and published briefs.
- [Recipes for writing, content, and media](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/creative-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that chain Bearly's image, video, text-to-speech, transcription, YouTube, grammar, and Run Python tools to make narrated clips, product visuals, show notes, trailers, quote cuts, and proofread drafts in one chat.
- [Recipes for founders, sales, and operations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/business-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that chain Bearly's Domain research, SEO data, People Search, Council of Experts, Document editor, Run Python, charts, Pages, and email for naming a product, meeting prep, contact lists, contract redlines, shareable dashboards, and search-gap content plans.
- [Recipes for automation and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/automation-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that combine Routines, Bearly Browser, Chrome control, Work locally, Computer Use, connectors, Deep Research, and email or text delivery, including what scheduled runs can and can't use and which steps need the desktop app.
- [Recipes for everyday life](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/everyday-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that chain Bearly's Maps, Weather, Best Pick, YouTube, Scholar, voice calls, text to speech, Run Python, Pages, and email for rides, trips, shopping, learning, reading health research, language practice, and splitting bills.
- [Recipes for teams and projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/team-recipes.md): Step-by-step recipes that combine projects, Always Search Documents, meeting transcripts, project sharing and locked settings, organization policies, Memories and chat management, Prompt Templates, email to teammates, and restricted Pages for shared knowledge bases, meeting recaps, and team rollouts.

### Workspace & Control

- [Projects: shared context, files, and settings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md): How Bearly projects work, including creating one, sidebar actions, roles and sharing, every Project Settings control, Docs & Data files and desktop folder sync, how project chats inherit settings and memory, password protection, archiving, and tested setups.
- [Run Bearly for an organization](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md): How to create and run a Bearly organization, including managed and unmanaged organizations, every console tab and setting (Overview, Agent access, Microsoft Entra and provisioning, Access banner, Members, Groups, Projects, Policies, Billing seats, Usage, Activity), and the complete policy control catalog.
- [Privacy and security controls](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md): Every privacy and security control in Bearly and what it changes, including Zero Data Retention only, end-to-end encryption with recovery phrase, device approval, and passkeys, personalization, memories and web search, project passwords, tool permissions, signed-in devices, logging out, account deletion, text messages, and admin-only controls.
- [Settings reference](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/settings-reference.md): Every pane and control in Bearly's Settings window, in the order the app shows them, with options, defaults, and what each control does, plus how to open Settings, read its save status, and find the panes that only appear on some platforms or plans.

### For AI & Agents

- [Guide for AI assistants](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/for-ai-assistants.md): How an AI assistant, including Bearly's own, should use this manual to answer questions about Bearly, check what a user's plan and device allow, and walk them through a task or a new combination of features.
- [Use Bearly from scripts and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/api-and-agents.md): How developers and AI agents use Bearly programmatically, including API keys, the Bearly API's endpoints, authentication, models, limits, and errors, the Bearly AI Models skill for scripts inside Bearly, links that open Bearly with a prompt, the Markdown versions of this manual, remote modules, and guidance for agents that operate Bearly's interface for a person.
- [Fix common problems](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/troubleshooting.md): What a Bearly message or symptom means and what to do about it — upload and PDF password errors, failed answers, a paused queue, usage limits and out-of-credits notices, hidden models, missing features, desktop-only tools, locked encrypted chats, routines that didn't run, connectors, email and text, Deep Research, Full access, Chrome control, voice, dictation, meetings, sharing, and sign-in.
- [Glossary](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/glossary.md): Plain definitions of the Bearly terms you meet in the app and in this manual, from Agentic (Compaction) and Bearly Fast to Full access, Routines, Voice Studio, and Zero Data Retention only, each with a link to the page that covers it.

# Bearly Manual

HTML: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/)

> The complete guide to using Bearly, covering every setting and capability, how features connect, and step-by-step recipes that combine them, for people, AI assistants, and agents.

This manual explains how to do anything in Bearly, from your first chat to workflows that chain research, data, media, agents, and automation. Each capability says when to use it, what it needs, what it produces, and what it combines with, and each recipe says why its steps work together, so you can adapt it to your own work.

New to Bearly? Read [What Bearly is](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/what-is-bearly.md), then [Tour the workspace](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/workspace-tour.md).

## I want to…

| Goal | Start here |
|---|---|
| Ask a question and get current answers with sources | [Research](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) |
| Get a long, cited research report on a topic | [Research → Deep Research](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) |
| Find and quote academic papers | [Research → Scholar](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) |
| Choose the one product worth buying | [Research → Best Pick](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) |
| Get the point of a YouTube video, or check its claims | [Videos and posts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/videos-and-posts.md) |
| Work with a PDF, document, or spreadsheet | [Files and documents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/documents.md) |
| Analyze data or make a chart | [Data and code](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/data-and-code.md) |
| Build a page, dashboard, or small tool and share a link | [Pages and Canvas](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/pages-and-canvas.md) |
| Make an image, a video, a voiceover, or a transcript | [Images, video, and audio](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/images-video-audio.md) |
| Record a meeting and use what was said | [Meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md) |
| Dictate, or talk to Bearly out loud | [Talk to Bearly](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md) |
| Let Bearly use a browser, a folder of files, or my screen | [Browser and computer](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md) |
| Connect Notion, Linear, GitHub, or my own server | [Connectors and skills](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/connectors-and-skills.md) |
| Run something every morning without asking | [Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md) |
| Get results by email or text | [Email and text](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/email-and-text.md) |
| Compare models, or ask several at once | [Choose and compare models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md) |
| Encrypt chats end to end, or use only zero-retention models | [Privacy and security](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md) |
| Find, organize, share, or export chats | [Organize, find, and share](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| Set a tone, language, or instructions for every chat | [Tune a chat](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/settings.md) |
| Share files and instructions across chats or with people | [Projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md) |
| Set up Bearly for a company | [Organizations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md) |
| Understand plans, credits, and limits | [Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md) |
| Call Bearly from code or another agent | [API and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/api-and-agents.md) |
| Combine features in a way I haven't seen | [Building blocks](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/building-blocks.md) |
| Fix something that isn't working | [Troubleshooting](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/troubleshooting.md) |

## Recipes

Each recipe chains several capabilities and explains the hand-off between them:

- [Research and analysis](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/research-recipes.md)
- [Writing, content, and media](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/creative-recipes.md)
- [Founders, sales, and operations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/business-recipes.md)
- [Automation and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/automation-recipes.md)
- [Everyday life](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/everyday-recipes.md)
- [Teams and projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/team-recipes.md)

## For AI assistants and agents

This manual is written to be read by software as well as people. Replace the trailing slash of any page address with `.md` for clean Markdown, start at [/manual.md](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual.md) for the directory, or read [/manual-full.txt](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual-full.txt) for everything in one file. [Guide for AI assistants](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/for-ai-assistants.md) explains how to use it to help someone, and [API and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/api-and-agents.md) covers programmatic access.

## Keeping it accurate

The manual is written from the current app and updated as features ship. The shorter [feature guides](https://bearly.ai/docs.md) cover single features, and the [changelog](https://bearly.ai/docs/changelog.md) lists every release. If something in the app looks different from what you read here, the app is right; tell us with **Give Feedback** in the account menu, or at support@bearly.ai.

# What Bearly is

HTML: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/what-is-bearly/](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/what-is-bearly/)

> The facts about Bearly in one place, including what it does, who it's for, which models and platforms it covers, how plans and privacy work, and where to go next.

Bearly is one app for Claude, GPT, Gemini, Grok, and more. You chat with the model you choose, and the same conversation can search the web with sources, read your files, run code, make charts, Pages, images, video, and audio, record meetings, and use a browser or your computer in the desktop app. Routines run a saved prompt on a schedule.

People use Bearly for real work: editors, founders, engineers, researchers, analysts, and writers, and the teams they work in.

## What you can do with it

| Area | What Bearly does | Manual page |
|---|---|---|
| Chat with any model | Pick a model per chat, rerun an answer with another model, or ask several at once with `/council` (Pro and up). | [Choose and compare models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md) |
| Research | Web search with sources, reading web pages, image search, Deep Research reports, academic papers with Scholar, product picks with Best Pick, people briefs, SEO and domain data, maps, and weather. | [Research](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) |
| Videos and posts | Summaries, chapters, quotes, cuts, and claim checks for YouTube videos; downloading video from supported sites; reading public posts on X. | [Videos and posts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/videos-and-posts.md) |
| Files | Read, search, summarize, and quote PDFs, office documents, spreadsheets, and more; create and edit office documents; fix grammar. | [Files and documents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/documents.md) |
| Data and code | Run Python on your files, and turn prepared data into charts. | [Data and code](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/data-and-code.md) |
| Publishing | Build Pages (documents, dashboards, small interactive tools) and publish them at a stable link. | [Pages and Canvas](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/pages-and-canvas.md) |
| Media | Generate and edit images, make short videos with sound, turn text into speech, and transcribe audio and video. | [Images, video, and audio](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/images-video-audio.md) |
| Voice and meetings | Dictate into the composer, talk with Bearly on a voice call, and record meetings with transcripts. | [Talk to Bearly](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md), [Meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md) |
| Agents | In the desktop app, Bearly can work in a browser, in a folder you choose, and on your screen, with your approval. | [Browser and computer](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md) |
| Your other apps | Connect services such as Notion, Linear, and GitHub, or your own servers, and add skills. | [Connectors and skills](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/connectors-and-skills.md) |
| Automation | Routines run a saved prompt on a schedule in the desktop app and can email or text you the result. | [Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md) |
| Shared work | Projects share files, instructions, and settings across chats and people; organizations manage members, policies, and billing. | [Projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md), [Organizations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md) |

Each of these is a building block whose output the next step can use. [Building blocks](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/combine/building-blocks.md) explains how they connect, and the recipe pages show tested combinations.

## Models

- Claude, GPT, Gemini, Grok, and more in one app. The model picker lists the models your organization allows, each labeled **ZDR** or **Standard** for whether its main route has zero data retention.
- **Bearly Fast**, **Bearly Pro**, and **Bearly Ultra** are Bearly's own choices, always kept up to date. Paid plans start new chats on Bearly Pro until 95% of the month's credits are used, then on Bearly Fast; the free plan starts on Bearly Fast.
- Some top models need the Pro or Analyst plan. Choosing one on a lower plan opens **Billing** with the plan it needs.
- The current list is in the picker, and in the [model catalog](https://bearly.ai/dashboard/model-catalog.json) for programs.

## Where it runs

Mac, Windows, Linux, iOS, web, and Chrome. There is no Android app; on Android, use Bearly in your phone's browser. Your chats sync across the web app, the desktop app, and the iOS app. The desktop app adds the features that need your computer: Bearly Browser, Work locally, Computer Use, installed skills, and running Routines. [Get Bearly](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly.md) has the full platform table.

## Plans

- Free to start with a personal email: a free plan with a small daily allowance and no credit card.
- Pro is $20 a month or $200 a year. Analyst is $60 a month, and Research is $100 a month (prices as of October 2026). Each paid plan gives you its price in AI credits every month, with no rate limits.
- Some features need a plan: Routines, Best Pick, and Council of Experts need Pro; Deep Research and People Search need Analyst; Deep Research's deeper Ultra tier needs Research.
- Organizations buy plan seats for their members on one company billing account.

[Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md) explains usage, boosts, and what happens at a limit.

## Privacy

Compared with ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Microsoft Copilot, Microsoft 365 Copilot, Perplexity, and Grok, Bearly is the most private of the major AI assistants, because of these measures:

- Bearly doesn't train AI models on your chats. No ads. We don't sell your data.
- AI labs never learn who is asking: no name, account, or IP address reaches them. Anything you type about yourself is still part of your message.
- Turn on **Zero Data Retention only** and Bearly shows only zero-retention models, and automatic fallbacks stay on them too. It covers chat models.
- Turn on end-to-end encryption and your new chats are encrypted on your devices. Bearly stores and syncs only the locked copies, and can't read those chats: not the messages, the titles, or the files. It's opt-in and in beta. Each message still goes to the model you chose to get an answer.
- Chats and files are encrypted at rest, each with its own key.
- Web search sends the search provider your query, never your name, account, or IP address. Image search runs on a provider that keeps nothing after it answers.
- Computer use runs on your device.

[Privacy and security](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md) lists every control and what it does.

## For organizations

Every model under one contract. Admins get feature-by-feature controls, model allowlists, spend limits for teams, groups, and members, lockable chat defaults, an access banner, and usage reports. Sign-in can run through Microsoft Entra single sign-on with SCIM provisioning. An audit log records admin, policy, membership, and billing changes. Admins can limit teams to zero-retention chat models with a model allowlist. Bearly is SOC 2 compliant. See [Organizations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md).

## For developers and agents

Bearly includes API access: create a key, call Bearly's models from your own code in the Chat Completions, Responses, or Messages request format, and spend the same credits, with no provider keys of your own. Every API request goes to a zero-retention route. This manual is also built for software: replace the trailing slash of any page address with `.md` for Markdown, read [/manual.md](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual.md) for the index, or [/manual-full.txt](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual-full.txt) for the whole manual in one file. See [API and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/api-and-agents.md).

## Company

Bearly first launched in November 2022. It is not open source. For help, use **Give Feedback** in the app's account menu, or email support@bearly.ai.

# Get Bearly on every device

HTML: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly/](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly/)

> Where Bearly runs (web app, phone browsers, desktop app, iOS app, Chrome extension), how to install and sign in on each, what the desktop app adds, and a table of which capabilities work on which platform.

Bearly runs on Mac, Windows, Linux, iOS, web, and Chrome. Sign in with the same account everywhere and your chats and projects follow you. Most of Bearly works the same on every platform; the work that touches your own computer, such as local files, a built-in browser, desktop control, and scheduled runs, needs the desktop app.

There is no Android app. On Android, use the web app in your phone's browser.

| Platform | What it is | How to get it |
|---|---|---|
| Web app | Bearly in a desktop browser | Go to `https://bearly.ai/dashboard` and sign in |
| Phone browser | The same web app on a phone or tablet, including Android | Open `https://bearly.ai/dashboard` in your phone's browser |
| Desktop app | Bearly for Mac, Windows, and Linux, plus local and browser tools | **App Settings** → **Get Desktop App**, or `bearly.ai/download/mac`, `/windows`, or `/linux` |
| iOS app | Bearly for iPhone and iPad (iOS 17 or later) | Search for Bearly in the App Store |
| Browser extension | **Bearly Companion** for Chrome: page summaries in a side panel, and the link that lets the desktop app control Chrome | Chrome Web Store, or **Install Chrome extension** in the desktop app |

## Web app

The web app is the full Bearly workspace in a desktop browser: chat with the models your plan includes, attach files, research, build Pages, generate images and video, record meetings from your microphone, set up projects and organizations, and manage routines.

**Use it when:** you're on a computer you don't own, you want nothing to install, or you only need chat, research, and documents.
**You need:** a current desktop browser and a Bearly account.

Features that need your computer show a prompt instead of failing. For example, **Work locally** opens **Get Bearly for desktop** with "Work locally lets Bearly read, edit, and run files in a folder you choose — available on the Bearly desktop app." Other desktop-only features show **Use the desktop app** with a **Get Bearly** button.

## Phone browsers

On a phone or a narrow window, the sidebar becomes a drawer: select **Open sidebar** to show it, and it closes when you pick a chat. Everything in the web app works except the desktop-only features, which show **Use Bearly on desktop**.

**Use it when:** you're on Android, or on an iPhone without the app.
**You need:** a current mobile browser.

- Connectors that need a service sign-in work in a normal mobile browser. On Android, they don't work in the in-app browser another app opens when you tap a link; those show **Web browser required**. Open the link in your browser app instead.
- You can add Bearly to your home screen from your browser's menu. It still needs a connection.
- The camera button **Take photo** appears on phones, tablets, and touch screens.

## Desktop app

The desktop app is Bearly in its own window, plus the tools that work on your computer. It loads Bearly from the internet, so it needs a connection. If it can't connect, it shows "We can't reach Bearly." and keeps retrying.

**Use it when:** you want Bearly to work with files on your computer, browse sites for you, control apps, run routines on a schedule, or record online meetings with computer audio.
**You need:** a Mac (one download works on every Mac processor type), a Windows x64 PC, or a Linux x64 computer.


**Mac**



1. In the web app, open the account menu at the bottom of the sidebar, select **App Settings**, then **Get Desktop App** and **Download for macOS**. You can also go to `bearly.ai/download/mac`.
2. Open the downloaded `.dmg` and drag **Bearly** into **Applications** — Bearly needs to run from Applications. If you open it from somewhere else, it offers **Move to Applications**.
3. Open Bearly and sign in.




**Windows**



1. In the web app, open **App Settings** → **Get Desktop App** → **Download for Windows**, or go to `bearly.ai/download/windows`.
2. Open the installer and follow the steps — it installs for your user account by default; you can choose to install it for everyone on the PC.
3. Open Bearly and sign in.




**Linux**



1. In the web app, open **App Settings** → **Get Desktop App** → **Download for Linux**, or go to `bearly.ai/download/linux`. This is an AppImage for x64 computers.
2. Mark the AppImage as executable if your system asks, then open it.
3. Sign in.






### What the desktop app adds

- **Work locally** — Bearly reads, edits, and runs files in a folder you pick, with your approval. The **Get Desktop App** pane calls this Cowork. See [Let Bearly use a browser, your files, and your computer](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md).
- **Bearly Browser** — a separate browser inside the app that chats can work in. Same page.
- **Control Chrome** — Bearly works in your everyday Chrome tabs through the Bearly Companion extension. Same page.
- **Computer Use** — Bearly clicks and types in the apps on your screen after you approve the task. Same page.
- **Installed skills and skill scripts**, and **npm MCP servers**. See [Connect apps and add skills](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/connectors-and-skills.md).
- **Running routines.** You can create routines anywhere, but they run only in the desktop app, on the device they're installed on, while it's open. See [Automate with Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md).
- **Online meeting** recording with computer audio and your microphone, on Windows and on macOS 13 or later. Linux records your microphone only. See [Meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md).
- **Local Directory Sync** for projects, and saving videos and transcripts over your own connection. See [Projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md) and [Videos and posts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/videos-and-posts.md).
- **Runtime** settings, where Bearly installs and checks the tools local work uses and keeps environment variables for local commands and skills.
- Full access for all of these tools. On other platforms it covers connectors only. See [Approvals, permissions, and Full access](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/permissions.md).

### Desktop settings

Open **App Settings** and look under the **Desktop** section. **Desktop**, **Runtime**, and **Chrome Extension** appear only in the desktop app; **Browser** appears there when Bearly Browser is available to your account.

| Setting | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Keyboard shortcut** | "Global hotkey to summon Bearly." The default is <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>P</kbd> on Mac and <kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>P</kbd> on Windows and Linux. It brings the Bearly window forward. Select **Change** to record a new one, or **Disable**. |
| **Always on top** | Keeps the window above other apps. It turns off when you quit. |
| **Auto-hide** | Hides Bearly when it loses focus. Bring it back with the keyboard shortcut. |
| **Launch on boot** | **Enabled** starts Bearly when you sign in to your computer. Off by default. |
| **Latest version** | Shows your installed version and whether a newer one exists. |

The app also puts an icon in the menu bar (Mac) or system tray (Windows and Linux); select it to show the window. Closing the window quits Bearly after **Quit Bearly?**. If Bearly is recording a meeting or saving a download, **Bearly is still working** offers **Keep Bearly open** or **Quit when finished**.

### Updates

The desktop app downloads updates in the background. When one is ready, **Update ready · Restart** appears at the top of the window; select it to restart into the new version. Most improvements arrive without a new download, because the app loads the current Bearly from the web. A feature that needs a newer app shows **Update Bearly**.

## iOS app

The iOS app is the same Bearly workspace for iPhone and iPad, with a few differences:

- **Sign-in is by email only.** Enter your email, select **Continue**, and type the code Bearly emails you. The account buttons shown on other platforms are hidden in the app.
- **Plans are bought through the App Store.** Plan cards read **Purchase** followed by the plan name. **Restore** checks the App Store for a subscription you already have. **Manage Account**, **Invoices**, and **Cancel** open the App Store's subscription settings. One-time Credit Boosts aren't sold in the app; buy them from the web or desktop app. If you subscribed through the App Store, other platforms show **Manage in iOS App**. See [Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md).
- **Connectors that need a service sign-in** show **Web browser required**. Connect and use those in a regular browser. Custom servers that use a token, custom headers, or no sign-in work in the app.
- **Desktop-only features** show a prompt to use Bearly on a computer. You can still create and edit routines in the app; they run in the desktop app.
- **Downloads** open the iOS share sheet. Links Bearly opens go to Safari or the app that owns the link.
- During a voice call, the screen stays on. Keep the app in front.

The app loads the current Bearly from the web, so most improvements arrive without an App Store update.

## Browser extension

**Bearly Companion** is Bearly's Chrome extension. It does two separate jobs:

- **On its own**, it adds a side panel that summarizes, explains, or answers questions about the page you're reading.
- **With the desktop app**, it lets a desktop chat work in your regular Chrome tabs. That connection needs no extension sign-in. See [Control Chrome](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md#control-chrome).

**Use it when:** you want a quick summary or answer about the page in front of you without opening Bearly.
**You need:** Chrome and a Bearly account.



1. Install **Bearly Companion** from the Chrome Web Store — in the desktop app, **App Settings** → **Desktop** → **Chrome Extension** → **Install Chrome extension** opens it.
2. Select the Bearly icon in the Chrome toolbar — the side panel opens.
3. Select **Sign in with Bearly**. In the window that opens, check the account shown under **Connect the Bearly extension**, then select **Continue** — the extension gets its own key, not your dashboard login.
4. Select **Allow website access** and approve Chrome's request — this one grant covers every site, and Bearly still reads a page only when you choose an action. The panel then shows **Ready for this page**.



**What you get:** above the composer, **Summarize** (main points, then key takeaways), **Explain** (the page in plain language), and **Explain selection** (needs selected text). The **Ask this page** box answers questions from the page; follow-ups can compare pages you visited in the same window. A small **Open Bearly actions** button at the bottom-right of pages runs the same actions and shows answers in the panel; its menu also has **Hide on this page**, **Don't show on this site**, and **Hide Forever**.
**Limits:** one conversation per Chrome window, kept until you select **Clear conversation**, close the window, quit Chrome, or disconnect. Questions can be up to 4,000 characters. Quick actions can't click, navigate, or use other tools, and they use one fixed model. The extension has no keyboard shortcut or right-click menu.

When you run an action, the extension sends the page's readable text (or only your selection, for **Explain selection**) and a picture of the visible part of the tab. Scroll away from anything you don't want in that picture.

Settings live behind the gear in the panel: **Response language**, **Quick actions** (which presets show, and their order), **Website access**, **Page button**, and **Disconnect Bearly**. Disconnecting removes Bearly from this browser; your account and work stay in Bearly. You can also remove the extension's key from any device under **App Settings** → **Devices** → **Browser extension and API keys**.

## Sign in

Bearly has no passwords. On the sign-in screen, enter your **Email** and select **Continue**; Bearly emails you a code made of three words. Type or paste it and select **Sign in**. Above the email field, the web and desktop apps also offer **Continue with** buttons for signing in with an existing account, including your organization's single sign-on where an administrator has set it up. The iOS app offers email sign-in only.

- **Desktop app:** account-button sign-in opens your default browser. Finish there, and the browser hands you back to the app. If it doesn't, the browser page offers **Retry desktop sign-in**.
- **Staying signed in:** you stay signed in until you sign out. **App Settings** → **Devices** lists every device signed in to your account, with **Sign out** on each and **Sign out all other devices**.
- **End-to-end encrypted chats** need more than sign-in: a new device needs approval from one you already use, your recovery phrase, or a passkey. See [Privacy and security](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md).

## What works where

"Desktop" means the desktop app on Mac, Windows, and Linux.

| Capability | Web app | Phone browser | Desktop app | iOS app | Extension |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Chat with models, files, and Pages | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Web research and Deep Research | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Images, video, and spoken audio | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Run Python (cloud) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Voice calls | Yes, not in Firefox | Where the browser supports it | Yes | Yes | No |
| Dictation | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Meeting recording with the microphone | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Online meeting with computer audio | No | No | Windows and macOS 13 or later | No | No |
| Projects | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Project Local Directory Sync | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| End-to-end encrypted chats | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Create and manage routines | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Run routines | No | No | Yes, on the installed device | No | No |
| Connectors with a service sign-in | Yes | Yes, not in Android in-app browsers | Yes | No | No |
| Connectors with a token, headers, or no sign-in | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| npm MCP servers | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Built-in skills (Visualize, Bearly AI Models) | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Installed skills and skill scripts | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Work locally | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Bearly Browser | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Control Chrome | No | No | Yes, with Chrome and the extension | No | Needed for it |
| Computer Use | No | No | Yes | No | No |
| Full access | Connectors only | Connectors only | All enabled tools | Connectors only | No |
| Send email and texts from a chat | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Summarize or ask about the page you're on | No | No | No | No | Yes |
| Buy or change a plan | Yes | Yes | Yes | App Store | No |
| Credit Boosts | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No |
| API keys | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| Global keyboard shortcut and tray icon | No | No | Yes | No | No |

Your organization's policy can turn many of these features off, and some features need a paid plan. See [Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md) and [Organizations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md).

## Related

- [Tour the workspace](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/workspace-tour.md) — where everything is once you're signed in.
- [Let Bearly use a browser, your files, and your computer](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md) — the desktop tools in detail.
- [Use Bearly from scripts and agents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/api-and-agents.md) — API keys and links that open Bearly with a prompt.

# Plans, credits, and limits

HTML: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits/](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits/)

> Bearly's plans and prices, which features each plan unlocks, how AI credits and the usage meter work, what you see at a limit, Credit Boost, and how to change, cancel, or restore a plan bought on the web, in the iOS app, or as an organization seat.

Every Bearly plan comes with an allowance of AI credits. This page covers what each plan includes, how usage is counted, what happens when you reach a limit, and where to change or cancel a plan.

## Plans at a glance

| Plan | Price | AI credits | What it adds |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Free** | $0 | A daily allowance and a monthly allowance | Light daily use of low-cost models, with a preview of most features |
| **Pro** | $20/mo | $20 of AI credits each month | Routines, Best Pick, Council, your choice of voice model, and models that need Pro |
| **Analyst** | $60/mo | $60 of AI credits each month | Everything in Pro, plus Deep Research, People Search, and models that need Analyst |
| **Research** | $100/mo | $100 of AI credits each month | Everything in Analyst, plus the Ultra tier of Deep Research |
| **Max** | Organization seats only | See your organization's billing | Everything in Research, for organizations that buy seats |

Yearly billing costs ten times the monthly price, so you pay for ten months and get twelve. The plan picker labels it **Yearly** **17% off**; organization billing calls it **Yearly · 2 months free**. Current prices are also on [bearly.ai/pricing](https://bearly.ai/pricing).

Max is not sold to personal accounts. An organization admin buys it as a seat in the organization's **Billing** tab. See [Run Bearly for an organization](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md).

## What each plan unlocks

| Feature | Minimum plan | What happens on a lower plan |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md) (scheduled chats) | Pro | Free accounts see the Routines page with an upgrade prompt |
| [Best Pick](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) (product research) | Pro | An upgrade card appears in the chat |
| [Council](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) (ask several models at once) | Pro | The chat shows "Council of Experts requires Pro or higher." with **Upgrade to Pro** |
| Choosing a voice model for [voice calls](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md) | Pro | Free calls use a fixed model, and the **Model** menu reads "Choosing a voice model needs a paid plan." |
| [Deep Research](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md), standard tier | Analyst | **Run Research** opens the upgrade flow |
| Deep Research, **Ultra** tier | Research | **Run Research** opens the upgrade flow |
| [People Search](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/research.md) | Analyst | An upgrade card appears in the chat |
| Some chat models | Pro or Analyst, per model | Choosing the model opens an upgrade prompt instead of switching |
| **Usage** pane in Settings | Any paid plan | Free accounts use the meter in the account menu |
| Credit Boost | Any paid plan, outside the iOS app | Not shown |

Web search, image search, reading web pages, Scholar, YouTube, X posts, SEO data, domain research, Maps, Weather, and document tools are on every plan. They draw on the same allowance as chat.

If you belong to an organization, its policy can still turn a feature off even when your plan includes it. See the [policy control catalog](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md).

## How usage is counted

Each request uses credits according to the work it takes. A long answer from a large model, a big file, or a research tool uses more than a short reply from a fast model. The percentage meter is a better guide than counting messages.

- **Paid plans** have a monthly allowance equal to the plan's price in AI credits. There is no separate daily limit.
- **Free** has a daily allowance that resets every day and a monthly allowance. Whichever you're closer to using up sets the meter.
- **Personal plans** (bought on the web or in the iOS app) reset on the first day of each calendar month (UTC), not on your billing date. The **Usage** pane shows the exact reset date.
- **Organization seats** follow the seat's billing period instead.
- If you move from a paid plan to Free partway through a month, what you used earlier that month counts toward Free's monthly allowance. Free can then stay unavailable until the next month.

### The usage meter

Open the account menu at the bottom of the sidebar. It shows your email, your plan badge, a meter bar, and a percentage. **+boost** appears beside the percentage while you have boost credits. On Free, the sidebar also shows **Unlock more AI — Go Pro**, which opens **Billing**.

### Which model new chats use

Free accounts start new chats on **Bearly Fast**. Paid accounts start on **Bearly Pro** until 95% of the allowance is used, then on **Bearly Fast**. A model you picked yourself is never changed. See [Models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md).

## What you see at a limit

When a request can't run, the chat shows a message and a button.

| Situation | Message | Button |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A feature needs a higher plan | "_Feature_ requires _Plan_ or higher." | **Upgrade to _Plan_** (or **View plans**) |
| Free monthly allowance used up | "You've used this month's free allowance. It resets on _date_. Upgrade to Pro to keep going now." | **Go Pro** |
| Free daily allowance used up | "You're out of credits. Please upgrade your plan to continue. Credits will reset tomorrow." | **Go Pro** |
| A Free request trips Bearly's free-plan checks | "This request tripped our free-plan checks. Go Pro to continue, or email support@bearly.ai if we've misread it." | **Go Pro** |
| Paid plan used up, boost available | "You've used your plan credits. Buy a boost to keep going, or upgrade your plan." The boost amounts appear as buttons. | **Upgrade Plan**, or **View Billing** if your plan can't be changed here |
| Paid plan and boost both used up | "You've used all your credits, including your boost. Buy another boost to keep going, or upgrade your plan." | **Upgrade Plan** or **View Billing** |
| Anything else | "You've used your plan credits. Open billing to see your options." | **View Billing** |

After you add credits or change plans, go back to the chat and send the request again.

## After paid credits run out

On Pro, Analyst, Research, and Max, running out of credits doesn't stop chat. Bearly keeps **Bearly Fast** and **Bearly Pro** available at no extra charge, up to a limited amount each day and each month. A notice above the composer reads "You're out of credits. Chat continues with Bearly Fast or Bearly Pro. Features like transcription need credits." with a **View billing** button.

- While this applies, the model picker shows only Bearly Fast and Bearly Pro.
- Paid tools still need credits.
- **Zero Data Retention only** and your organization's model rules still apply.
- If your organization's spending limit stopped you, chat continues on **Bearly Fast** only. The notice reads "You've used your team allowance. Chat continues with Bearly Fast until it resets." with **View usage**. Your admin's limit still applies to every other model.
- Free accounts don't get this. They wait for the reset or upgrade.

When your credits reset or you add more, the full model list comes back.

## Credit Boost

A Credit Boost buys one-time credits on top of your plan. Use it when one month runs heavier than usual.

**You need:** a paid plan whose billing you manage yourself. Boosts aren't offered on Free, on billing managed by a team admin, or inside the iOS app. If you subscribe through the App Store, you can still buy a boost from Bearly on the web or the desktop app.



1. Open the account menu and select **Billing & Pro**, or select **Buy Boost** in **Usage** — this opens **Settings** → **Billing**.
2. Under **Credit Boost**, pick an amount — each option shows the credits you get and the price you pay before you check out.
3. Complete checkout, then open **Usage** — **Boost remaining** shows the new balance. In **Billing**, the **Credit Boost** card shows it as "_amount_ remaining".



**What you get:** extra credits on top of your plan allowance. The meter shows them as a separate boost segment, and the account menu shows **+boost**.

**Limits:** boosts expire at the end of the calendar month, or at the end of the seat's billing period for an organization seat. If boosts become routine, the card may suggest a plan that includes more credits each month.

## The Billing pane

Open it from the account menu with **Billing & Pro**, or from **Settings** → **Billing**. It shows, in order: seat cards if an organization gives you a seat, the **Current Plan** card, **Credit Boost** on eligible plans, and the plan picker.

### Current Plan

The card shows your plan badge and, when a cancellation is scheduled, **Cancels on** with the date. It offers these buttons, and what each one does depends on where you bought the plan (see the table below):

- **Manage Account** — payment method and account details.
- **Invoices** — past invoices.
- **Restore** — re-checks your subscription, for example after buying on another device.
- **Cancel** — stops renewal. Shown on a paid plan with no cancellation scheduled.
- **Reactivate** — undoes a scheduled cancellation. An organization seat shows **Reinstate** instead.

### The plan picker

Choose **Monthly** or **Yearly**, then compare the cards for **Pro**, **Analyst**, and **Research**. A **Free** card appears when you're on a paid plan. Your plan is marked **CURRENT** and its button reads **Current Plan**. Other buttons read **Upgrade to _Plan_** (from Free), **Switch to _Plan_**, or **Purchase _Plan_** in the iOS app. Choosing **Free** on a plan you bought on the web cancels it.

### Actions by where you bought the plan

| Bought through | Change plan | Cancel | Manage Account and Invoices | Restore |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| The web | Opens a secure billing page | **Cancel** opens the billing page's cancel flow. The plan stays until the end of the period. | Open the billing page | Refreshes your plan. If none is found: "No subscription found for this account". Sign in with the account that has the subscription. |
| The iOS App Store | Purchase in the iOS app. Bearly shows "Setting up your pro account..." while it confirms. | In the iOS app, opens Apple's subscription management | In the iOS app, open Apple's subscription management | Checks Apple for your purchase |
| An organization seat | Only an organization admin can change a seat, in the organization's **Billing** tab. An admin who holds a seat can also use these buttons in their own **Billing** pane. | For an admin: ends the seat at the end of its billing period, and **Reinstate** keeps it | For an admin: the organization's payment and invoice page | For an admin: reinstates an ending seat |
| Billing managed by a team admin | Not available | Not available | Not available | Not available |

Outside the iOS app, an App Store subscription's plan buttons read **Manage in iOS App** and can't be selected: "Plan changes for App Store subscriptions happen in the iOS app." Accounts whose billing a team admin manages see **Team-Managed Billing**: "This account's billing is managed by a team admin. Contact support for billing assistance." with **Contact Support**.

### When you have a seat and a personal plan

- A seat card reads "_Organization_ gives you a _Plan_ seat".
- If both are paid, **You have two paid plans** appears with **End personal plan**. It ends the personal plan at the end of its own billing period. Nothing is canceled automatically.
- If your personal plan is higher, **Your personal plan still provides more access** explains: "Bearly uses the higher plan and never adds the allowances together."
- If the organization's payment is past due: "Team seat access is blocked until an admin fixes the payment. Every team admin has been notified."
- **Add teammates** → **Open teams** takes you to the Organizations console, where an organization buys seats on its own billing account.

## Cancel, reactivate, or restore



1. Open **Settings** → **Billing** — the **Current Plan** card shows your options.
2. Select **Cancel** — on the web this opens the billing page's cancel flow; in the iOS app it opens Apple's subscription management.
3. Return to **Billing** — the card shows **Cancels on** and the end date. You keep the plan until then.
4. To keep the plan after all, select **Reactivate** before that date.



If you subscribed in the iOS app, cancel from the iOS app. On other devices, Bearly says "You must be in the iOS app to manage your subscription." If a purchase doesn't show up, sign in with the Bearly account that bought it and select **Restore**.

> **Caution — Deleting your account and subscriptions**
> Deleting your Bearly account cancels a web subscription when the deletion runs, not when you schedule it, and doesn't cancel an App Store subscription. Unused time and credits aren't refunded. See [Delete your account](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md).


## The Usage pane

Paid plans get **Settings** → **Usage**. Free accounts don't have this pane.

**Current Period** shows your plan badge, the percentage used, and when credits reset ("Credits reset in _N_ days"). It also shows **Used this month**, **Monthly limit**, **Today**, and **Boost remaining** when you have a boost. **Manage** and **Buy Boost** both open **Billing**.

**Usage by service** lists the models and paid data tools you used in a month, with **Service**, **Calls**, and **Cost** columns and a **Total** row. Paid data tools are named by what they did, such as "Maps · place search" or "Weather · daily forecast". Use the month arrows to go back as far as the month you joined. An empty month reads "No usage this month."

If an organization admin set a spending limit for you, the limit shown here can reflect it.

## Related

- [Run Bearly for an organization](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md) — seats, spending limits, and usage reports for admins
- [Models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md) — which models your plan offers
- [Manage billing and review usage](https://bearly.ai/docs/account/billing-usage.md) and [Cancel your Bearly subscription](https://bearly.ai/docs/account/cancel-subscription.md)
- [Troubleshooting](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/troubleshooting.md)

# Tour the workspace

HTML: [https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/workspace-tour/](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/workspace-tour/)

> A map of Bearly's screens and where each control lives — the sidebar and account menu, the New Chat launcher, the chat header, thread, composer, and context strip, the command menu, the Settings window, the other pages, and what changes in the desktop app, on phones, and in the iOS app.

The web app, a phone browser, the desktop app, and the iOS app all run the same Bearly app, so the screens on this page look alike everywhere. Each area below names its controls as the app shows them and links to the page that covers it in depth. The browser extension is separate: it has its own side panel for quick page actions, covered in [Get Bearly](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly.md).

## The sidebar

The column on the left holds navigation, your projects, and your chats. In a narrow window or on a phone it becomes a drawer: tap **Open sidebar** to show it, and picking an item closes it again.

From top to bottom:

| Area | What it does | More |
|---|---|---|
| **Chat** | Your chats | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| **Prompt Templates** | Saved prompts with fill-in fields that run as a form in any chat | [Write, attach, and send](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/compose.md) |
| **Routines** | Prompts that run on a schedule. Pro and higher; on Free it shows only if you already have routines | [Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md) |
| **Meetings and Recordings** | Record a meeting or a voice note and get a transcript | [Meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md) |
| Magnifier (**Search conversations**) | Opens **Search chats and files...**, with **Chats** and **Files** tabs. Shortcut <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>F</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>F</kbd> on Windows and Linux) | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| **New Chat** | Opens the New Chat launcher, outside any project | [The New Chat launcher](#the-new-chat-launcher) |
| **Needs attention** | Appears with a count when other chats are waiting for an approval or have replies you haven't read. Select it to see **Awaiting approval** and **Unread**; select it again to show all chats | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| **Projects** | Your projects. The header has **Collapse all projects** and **New project**; each project opens to **Settings**, **Docs & Data**, a button to start a chat, and its chats | [Projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md) |
| **Pinned** | Chats you pinned | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| Date groups | Every other chat by last activity: **Today**, **This Week**, **This Month**, then each earlier month of this year, then earlier years | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| **Archives** | Shows how many chats you've archived and opens the archive list | [Organize chats](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/organize.md) |
| Account menu | The bottom row, with your email, plan badge, and usage meter | [The account menu](#the-account-menu) |

**Signs on a chat row.** An accent bar on the left means unread. A spinner means Bearly is working. A shield means the chat is waiting for your approval. A lock reads "Approve this device to open this chat": it's an end-to-end encrypted chat this device can't open yet.

**More actions** (the **…** on a chat row, shown on hover, and always on touch screens and for the open chat) offers **Rename**, **Pin** or **Unpin**, **Move to project...**, **Archive** or **Unarchive**, **Mark unread** or **Mark read**, **Delete**, and **Select** for choosing several chats at once. With a mouse, you can also drag a chat onto a project to move it there.

### The account menu

Select the bottom row of the sidebar. The row shows your email, your plan, and a usage meter with a percentage, plus "+boost" while a Credit Boost is active.

- **App Settings** opens the Settings window where you left it.
- **Billing & Pro** opens **Settings** → **Billing**.
- **Developers** opens **Settings** → **API Keys**. It appears when API keys or personal remote modules are allowed for your account.
- **Organizations** opens the organizations console.
- **Follow us on X**, **Give Feedback**, and **Logout**.

On the Free plan, an **Unlock more AI — Go Pro** banner in the sidebar opens **Billing**. See [Plans, credits, and limits](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/plans-and-credits.md).

## The New Chat launcher

The launcher is the screen a new chat opens on. Get there with **New Chat** in the sidebar, <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>N</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>N</kbd> on Windows and Linux), or **New** in the command menu. From inside a project chat, the shortcut and **New** start the new chat in the same project; the sidebar button starts it outside any project.

- **Headline.** A short line at the top that changes with the date and the time of day. The default is "Ready when you are."
- **Composer.** The same composer as in a chat, with the cursor already in it. Its placeholder rotates; the default is "How can I help today?" If the chat can't be prepared, your draft stays put and a notice offers **Try again**.
- **Project chip.** "Start this chat in a project" opens a picker with "Find or create a project...", **Recent**, **All projects**, **New project**, and **No project**. The chip then reads "Starting in project" with the project's name. The project is fixed once the chat starts.
- **Encryption.** Before you set up end-to-end encryption, a dismissible "Set up end-to-end encryption" prompt sits under the composer. After setup, a lock in the strip switches this draft between end-to-end and standard encryption. It can't change while the draft has attachments. See [Privacy and security](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/privacy-and-security.md).
- **Work locally.** In the desktop app, the chip shows but stays disabled until the chat loads ("Available once the chat loads").
- **Tips.** Short notes appear under the composer, labeled **Tip**, **Note**, **What's new**, or **Required**, and you get at most one tip a day. Dismissing one hides tips for the rest of the day on all your devices. A **Required** notice blocks sending until you select **Acknowledge**.

When a release newer than the last one you saw on this device is out, **What's New** opens on its own, with **Highlights**, **Also new**, and **Fixes & improvements**. To open it yourself, use the command menu → **Show all...** → **Show Release Notes**.

## The chat screen

### Header

From left to right:

- **Project and title.** In a project chat, the project's name comes first. Select the title to rename the chat: <kbd>Enter</kbd> saves, <kbd>Esc</kbd> reverts.
- **Browse files.** Appears once the chat has files. It opens a drawer with **Project**, **Uploaded**, and **Generated** tabs. See [Documents](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/documents.md).
- **New chat.** A pen icon, shown only while the sidebar is closed.
- **Share.** Turns the substance of the conversation into a Page you review before publishing. See [Pages and Canvas](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/pages-and-canvas.md).
- **Open Browser.** In the desktop app, a globe that opens Bearly Browser. See [Browser and computer use](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md).
- **…** opens the command menu. See [The command menu](#the-command-menu).

### Thread

- **Messages.** Your messages and Bearly's answers. Controls sit under each one: they appear on hover with a mouse and stay visible on touch screens and in narrow windows. Under your messages: **Copy**, **Edit Query**, **Rerun with same model**, the **Rerun** model menu, a version switcher such as ‹ 2 / 3 ›, **Query info**, and **Delete Query**. Answers have most of the same, plus **Read aloud** on text-only answers and **Edit in Canvas**. See [Work with answers](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/answers.md).
- **Thinking.** A model's reasoning, folded into collapsible **Thinking** steps.
- **Tool steps.** Each tool Bearly uses shows as a collapsible row with **Input**, **Result**, and, if something failed, **Error**.
- **Approval cards.** When a tool needs your OK, a card shows what it wants to do, with **Deny**, **Allow**, and an **Approval duration** choice. See [Permissions](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/permissions.md).
- **Result cards.** Interactive results such as a Deep Research report, a **Chart**, a YouTube video card, or the image editor.
- **Timeline rail.** In windows at least 1,280 pixels wide, chats with 5 or more prompts get a slim rail of ticks on the right edge, one per prompt. Hover a tick for "Prompt N of M" and a preview; select it to jump. Ticks marked **Chapter** label a stretch of the conversation. <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>↑</kbd> and <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>↓</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> on Windows and Linux) jump between prompts.
- **Scroll to bottom.** A button that appears when you've scrolled up.

### Composer

The message box at the bottom. Its placeholder reads "How can I help? Type / or @ for tools". See [Write, attach, and send](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/compose.md).

- **Up next.** Above the box, messages you queued while Bearly was working.
- **Left of the toolbar:** **+** (**Attach files** and **Share your screen**); **Take photo** on touch screens; the sliders button, which opens this chat's **Web**, **Style**, **Scholar**, **Reasoning**, **Memories**, and **History** settings plus **Chat Settings**. See [Tune a chat](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/settings.md).
- **Right of the toolbar:** the model button (**Select model**), which shows the current model's short name; **Start voice call**, with a sound-wave icon; and the send button, which reads **Send message**, **Queue message** while Bearly works and you have a draft, or **Stop** while Bearly works and the box is empty. See [Choose and compare models](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/models.md) and [Voice](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md).
- **Top right of the box:** the dictation microphone. See [Voice](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/voice.md).

### Context strip

A row of chips under the message box:

- **Project.** The chat's project, if it has one.
- **Encryption lock.** "End-to-end encrypted" or "Standard encryption". If your organization requires device-only encryption, it reads "Local key encryption" and can't be turned off.
- **Work locally.** Desktop app only. It shows the working folder's name, or "Work locally" with "· Off". Its menu lists recent folders, **Choose folder…**, **Clear folder**, and **Off for this chat** or **Turn on for this chat**. In the web app it opens the desktop download pane; on phones and in the iOS app it's hidden. See [Browser and computer use](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/browser-and-computer.md).
- **Connectors.** The connected service's name, or a count such as "2 connectors". Each row reads **Active**, **Off**, **Connect**, or **Retry**, and **Manage connectors...** opens settings. See [Connectors and skills](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/connectors-and-skills.md).
- **Permissions.** Reads **Permissions**, or **Full access** while Full access is on, with a **Paused** badge if it's paused. It opens **Standard** and **Full access**, your saved approvals with **Revoke**, **Revoke all saved approvals**, and **Clear all permissions**. See [Permissions](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/permissions.md).

While you edit an earlier message, the strip shows **Editing message** with **Cancel**. While Computer Use works in the desktop app, a **Computer** chip shows its status and a **Stop** action.

### The command menu

Press <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>K</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>K</kbd> on Windows and Linux) or select **…** in the header. It shows the chat's title, a **Search...** box, and two groups:

- **Actions:** **New**, **Delete & New**, **Delete**, **Rename**, **Refresh Chat**, **Generate New Name**, **Download as Markdown**, **New Routine** (Pro and higher), and **Show all...**, which adds **Show Release Notes**.
- **Switch Chats:** every chat, including project chats.

See [Keyboard shortcuts](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/shortcuts.md).

## The Settings window

Open it with <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>,</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>,</kbd> on Windows and Linux), the account menu → **App Settings**, or the sliders button → **Chat Settings**. It opens on the pane you used last. The header shows **Saving...**, **Saved**, or **Not saved**; if a save fails, the window stays open showing **Not saved**. In a narrow window, a dropdown at the top replaces the list of panes.

| Section | Panes | When a pane shows |
|---|---|---|
| **Chats** | **This Chat**, **New Chats**, **Personalization**, **Voice**, **Dictation** | In a chat. **Voice** and **Dictation** need those features allowed for your account |
| **Settings** | **Appearance**, **Get Desktop App**, **Skills**, **Connectors**, **Privacy & Security**, **Billing**, **Usage**, **Phone & SMS**, **Account**, **Devices** | **Get Desktop App** is hidden inside the desktop app. **Usage** is on paid plans. **Connectors** and **Phone & SMS** need those features allowed |
| **Desktop** | **Desktop**, **Browser**, **Runtime**, **Chrome Extension** | Desktop app only. **Browser** appears when Bearly Browser is available to your account |
| **Developer** | **API Keys**, **Modules** | **API Keys** when developer API access is allowed, which it is by default. **Modules** only when an organization admin allows personal remote modules |

What each pane holds:

- **This Chat**: settings for this conversation only: **Model**, **Language**, **Response Style**, **Memories**, **Web Search**, **Reasoning Effort**, **Instructions**, **Temperature**, and **Reset all to defaults**.
- **New Chats**: the same settings as defaults for new chats, with **Recommended** as a model choice. Project chats use the project's settings instead.
- **Personalization**: what you write in **Name**, **Occupation**, and **About you**, added to your chats only while the switch is on. See [Personalization](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/chat/settings.md#personalization).
- **Voice**: the model voice calls use. **Dictation**: **Enable dictation**, the push-to-talk shortcut, what happens after dictation, transcript cleanup, and words and names.
- **Appearance**: **Theme**, **Font**, and **Font size**, for this device only.
- **Get Desktop App**: downloads for Mac, Windows, and Linux.
- **Skills**: the **Skill Store** and your installed skills. Installing needs the desktop app.
- **Connectors**: **Your connectors**, **Built-in connectors**, and **Add custom MCP server**.
- **Privacy & Security**: **Zero Data Retention only**, **End-to-end encryption**, and **Start new chats in Full access**, among others.
- **Billing**: **Current Plan**, **Credit Boost**, and the plan picker. **Usage**: **Current Period** and **Usage by service**.
- **Phone & SMS**: add and verify a US or Canadian mobile number for texts and codes, or turn texts off.
- **Account**: **Version**, **Lock all data**, **Delete account**, and **Log out**.
- **Devices**: where you're signed in, **Sign out all other devices**, and **Browser extension and API keys**.
- **Desktop**: **Keyboard shortcut**, **Always on top**, **Auto-hide**, **Launch on boot**, and **Latest version**.
- **Browser**: **Enable Browser**, **Allow chats to use Browser**, **Block ads and trackers**, **Import browser cookies**, **Reset open tabs**, and **Delete browser data**.
- **Runtime**: tools the desktop app installs for local work, the Windows Computer Use helper, and environment variables for local commands.
- **Chrome Extension**: **Control Chrome from chat** and the connection status.
- **API Keys**: keys for calling Bearly models from your own code. **Modules**: remote modules that add tools from your own server.

Every setting, with its options and defaults, is in the [Settings reference](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/settings-reference.md).

## Other pages

- **Prompt Templates.** Make your first with **Create Prompt Template**, or later with **New Prompt**, which opens the **AI Prompt Builder** with **Popular Ideas**. The editor has **Basic Information**, **Template Design**, and **Field Configuration**; your list is **My Prompts**.
- **Routines.** A **Create a routine** box where you describe the result and the timing, then **Draft routine**; **Start from scratch** for a blank editor; your routines with their badges; and a **Next seven days** strip. See [Routines](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/routines.md).
- **Meetings and Recordings.** Source cards for **Online meeting** (desktop app) and **In person or voice note**, **Start recording**, and your recordings with their transcripts. See [Meetings](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/capabilities/meetings.md).
- **Project Settings and Docs & Data.** Open from a project in the sidebar. Settings covers sharing, encryption, the project's chat settings, skills, and, in the desktop app, **Local Directory Sync**. Docs & Data holds the project's files. See [Projects](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/projects.md).
- **Organizations.** The account menu → **Organizations**. Everyone sees **Overview**, **Members**, and **Groups**. Admins also see **Policies**, **Projects**, and **Activity**, plus **Billing** and **Usage** where they apply. See [Organizations](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/organizations.md).

## In the desktop app

The desktop app for Mac, Windows, and Linux has the same screens plus a few of its own:

- **Summon Bearly from anywhere** with <kbd>⌘</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>P</kbd> (<kbd>Ctrl</kbd> <kbd>Shift</kbd> <kbd>P</kbd> on Windows and Linux). Change it in **Settings** → **Desktop** → **Keyboard shortcut**.
- **The menu bar or tray icon** shows the window when you click it.
- **Closing the window quits Bearly** after asking **Quit Bearly?**. While it's recording a meeting or saving a Browser download to a chat, it asks **Bearly is still working** instead, with **Keep Bearly open** and **Quit when finished**.
- **Updates** download in the background; an **Update ready · Restart** pill at the top installs them.
- **Desktop-only areas**: the **Work locally** chip, **Open Browser**, the **Desktop** settings section, and running routines.

See [Get Bearly](https://bearly.ai/docs/manual/get-bearly.md).

## On phones and in the iOS app

There's no Android app; on Android, use Bearly in your phone's browser. Bearly runs on Mac, Windows, Linux, iOS, web, and Chrome. On a phone-sized screen, Bearly tells the AI you're on a phone and asks it to keep answers brief and prefer lists over tables.

| Area | Phone browser | iOS app |
|---|---|---|
| Sidebar | A drawer: tap **Open sidebar** | Same |
| Sending | <kbd>Enter</kbd> adds a new line; tap the send button | Same |
| Photos | **Take photo** camera button in the toolbar | Same; opens the rear camera |
| Message controls | Always visible | Same |
| Moving chats | No dragging; use **More actions** → **Move to project...** | Same |
| Timeline rail | Not shown | Not shown |
| Context strip | Shown under the message box | **Permissions** moves to the send row; the strip appears only while you edit a message |
| **Work locally** | Hidden | Hidden |
| Sign-in | All sign-in options | Email code only |
| Connectors that need a service sign-in | Work in a normal phone browser, but not in an Android app's built-in browser | Show **Web browser required**; set them up in a browser |
| Desktop-only features | A prompt to use Bearly on a computer | Same |
| Routines | Create and manage; they run in the desktop app ("Runs in the desktop app. **Get it**") | Same |
| Meetings | **In person or voice note** only | Same |
| Voice calls | Where the browser supports them; the screen can lock mid-call | The screen stays on during a call; keep the app in front |
| PDFs | Preview in the app | **Open PDF** opens them outside the app |
| Downloads | A normal browser download | The iOS share sheet |
| Billing | Web billing | App Store purchase and **Restore**; Credit Boost isn't offered in the iOS app |
| **Get Desktop App** | **Choose a computer** | Same |

The iOS app has no share extension, widgets, or push notifications. Most Bearly updates reach the iOS app without an App Store update, because the app loads the live Bearly app; changes to the app's own features, such as purchases, need a new App Store version.
